### [1] [ScanTwin: Simulating Performance Regressions Without Access to Tenant Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29093)
*Donghyun Sohn,Jennie Rogers*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ScanTwin是一个轻量级框架，从Parquet文件页脚提取每行组的边界值和压缩大小等物理属性，通过边界参数化实现ε-差分隐私保护，生成能还原引擎扫描行为（尤其是行组剪枝）的合成数据。在TPC-H和SSB基准测试中，无隐私保护时剪枝误差为0%、字节误差<1%；在ε=5下，高选择性查询的剪枝误差<8.5%，在DuckDB上的扫描时间与原始数据高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据平台中，开发者在特定租户数据集上遇到性能回退问题，但由于保密约束无法访问原始数据，导致难以本地复现。现有合成数据方法只关注统计属性（如匹配数据分布或提高查询精度），忽略了控制引擎扫描行为（如行组剪枝）的物理属性。

Method: 从Parquet文件页脚提取每行组的sketch（边界值和压缩大小），通过边界参数化方法施加ε-差分隐私保护，生成能保留物理属性的合成数据描述。

Result: 在TPC-H和SSB（600万行）上：ε=∞时剪枝误差0%、字节误差<1%；ε=5时，高选择性查询（>30%）的剪枝误差低于8.5%；在DuckDB上的逐查询扫描时间与原始数据高度一致。

Conclusion: ScanTwin有效解决了云数据平台中因数据隐私限制而无法复现性能回退的问题，通过仅提取和发布物理属性sketch，在保护隐私的同时高度还原了原始数据的扫描行为。

Abstract: In cloud data platforms, developers often encounter performance regressions that occur in specific tenant datasets. However, due to confidentiality constraints, they cannot access the original data, which makes it difficult to reproduce these regressions locally. Current methods for synthetic data usually focus on statistical properties, such as matching data distributions or improving query accuracy. However, they overlook the physical properties that control how the engine behaves during scans, including row-group pruning.
  We propose ScanTwin, a lightweight framework that extracts a per-row-group sketch from the Parquet footer, including boundary values and compressed sizes, and releases them under $\varepsilon$-differential privacy using a boundary parameterization. On TPC-H and SSB (6M rows), ScanTwin achieves 0% pruning error and less than 1% byte error at $\varepsilon{=}\infty$. Under $\varepsilon{=}5$, high-selectivity queries ($>$30%) incur below 8.5% pruning error on both datasets, and per-query scan timing on DuckDB closely tracks the original.

</details>


### [2] [One Ring to Shuffle Them All: Scalable Intra-Process Data Redistribution with Ring-Buffer Shuffle in Redpanda Oxla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29099)
*Adam Szymański,Tyler Akidau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无锁环形缓冲区的流式洗牌（ring-buffer streaming shuffle）设计，解决了传统批处理洗牌（batch partitioning）和通道流式洗牌（channel-based streaming）在多核CPU下扩展性差的问题。该设计通过无锁原子槽位分配实现均摊O(1)同步开销，内存消耗O(M)与输入规模无关。在72核至192核的多代硬件上，性能优于现有方案44%至300%以上，已在Redpanda的Oxla查询引擎中生产运行两年。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代服务器CPU每插槽核心数已达数十至数百核，并行查询引擎在线程间重新分配数据的现有方案存在根本性扩展瓶颈：批处理洗牌虽避免热路径跨线程同步，但需物化所有中间数据、引入全局生产者/消费者屏障且缓存局部性差；通道流式洗牌虽避免物化，但每通道同步开销随核心数剧增。核心数增长使得这些架构取舍日益阻碍引擎充分利用现代硬件。

Method: 设计无锁环形缓冲区流式洗牌：通过无锁原子操作将槽位分配到固定大小的批组中，实现均摊O(1)同步开销每批次，内存消耗O(M)与输入规模无关。该设计已在Redpanda的Oxla查询引擎中实现并生产运行两年。

Result: 在72核单插槽系统上，环形缓冲区较通道流式提升44%、较批处理洗牌提升79%；192核时优势分别超100%和300%。但在具有多分区L3缓存的chiplet架构上，共享原子计数器成为跨晶片瓶颈，通道流式仍具竞争力。端到端TPC-H（21查询）和ClickBench（43查询）在Graviton4上的评估显示优势依赖工作负载形状。

Conclusion: 环形缓冲区流式洗牌设计在多核扩展性上显著优于传统方案，但chiplet架构下共享原子计数器存在跨晶片瓶颈；实际优势与工作负载形状相关，需根据硬件拓扑和查询特性选择合适的洗牌策略。

Abstract: As server CPUs scale to dozens and now hundreds of cores per socket, parallel query engines must rethink how they redistribute data between threads. Partitioned operators such as hash joins and aggregations require frequent data redistribution across threads, yet existing intra-process shuffle designs fundamentally fail to scale with core count: batch partitioning avoids cross-thread synchronization in the hot path but materializes all intermediate data, introduces a global producer/consumer barrier, and requires a consumption approach with low cache locality, while channel-based streaming avoids materialization but incurs per-channel synchronization that scales poorly with core count. As core counts rise, these architectural tradeoffs increasingly prevent engines from fully utilizing modern hardware.
  We present a ring-buffer streaming shuffle design that addresses these shortcomings through lock-free atomic slot acquisition into fixed-size batch groups, achieving amortized O(1) synchronization cost per batch and O(M) memory independent of input size. Ring-buffer shuffle has been implemented in Redpanda's Oxla query engine for two years, where it currently powers production queries for Redpanda SQL users.
  We evaluate all three approaches on a 72-core NVIDIA GraceHopper, a 192-core dual-socket AWS Graviton4, and a 96-core (192-thread) AMD EPYC. On a 72-core single-socket system the ring buffer outperforms channel streaming by up to 44% and batch partitioning by up to 79%; at 192 cores the advantage over channel grows to over 100% and over 300% versus batch partitioning. Even so, on chiplet architectures with many partitioned L3 caches, the shared atomic counter becomes a cross-die bottleneck and channel-based streaming remains competitive. End-to-end Graviton4 evaluation on TPC-H (21 queries) and ClickBench (43 queries) shows the advantage is workload-shape-dependent.

</details>


### [3] [Towards Reliable Agentic Progressive Text-to-Visualization with Verification Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29692)
*Xu Wenxin,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiaoyong Wei,Haoyang Li,Hwanhee Kim,Yuanfeng Song,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种渐进式多轮交互范式PMVis用于文本到可视化任务，构建了首个支持渐进式迭代查询的数据集PMVisBench，并设计了基于多智能体框架的PMVisAgent，通过验证与修复机制减轻错误累积，在单表和多表场景下执行准确率分别提升17.57%和23.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到可视化方法采用单轮范式，要求用户一次性指定所有可视化细节，导致认知负荷过重且容易生成错误可视化。

Method: 提出PMVis渐进式多轮交互范式；构建PMVisBench数据集（通过VQL简化和NLQ重构，并设置规则约束确保中间VQL有效）；设计PMVisAgent多智能体框架（包含User、System和Validation Agent，通过ReAct风格工具循环进行验证与修复）。

Result: 在PMVisBench上，PMVisAgent在单表和多表设置下执行准确率分别提升17.57%和23.21%，消融实验验证了渐进式交互与澄清机制的重要性。

Conclusion: 渐进式多轮交互范式能有效降低用户认知负荷并提升文本到可视化的准确率，多智能体验证修复机制可缓解多轮交互中的错误累积问题。

Abstract: Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) translates natural language queries into visualization query languages, enabling non-expert users to perform data analysis. However, most existing methods follow a one-shot paradigm that requires users to specify all visualization details in a single round, often leading to cognitive overload and incorrect visualizations. In this paper, we propose PMVis, a progressive multi-turn paradigm for text-to-vis, where users' intents are refined through multi-turn interactions. To support research in this paradigm, we construct PMVisBench, the first dataset designed to capture the progressive and iterative nature of real-world user queries. It is built through VQL simplification and NLQ reconstruction, with explicit rule constraints to ensure each intermediate VQL remains valid and meaningful. Building upon PMVis, we further introduce PMVisAgent, an agent-based framework that simulates realistic user-system dialogues. PMVisAgent consists of a User, a System, and a Validation Agent that performs verification and repair via a ReAct-style tool-use loop to mitigate error accumulation across rounds, with explicit interaction and verification rules to ensure reliability of the multi-agent system. Extensive experiments on PMVisBench demonstrate that PMVisAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-vis baselines. It achieves up to 17.57\% and 23.21\% improvements in execution accuracy in single-table and multi-table settings, respectively, while ablation studies confirm the importance of combining progressive interaction with clarification. The code is available at https://github.com/wxxv/PMVis.

</details>

### [4] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"动态对称性"概念，通过衡量机器人质心加速度均匀性的"动态各向同性"指标，在超过1000种仿真形态中发现高动态对称性可一致提升轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效。开发了Argus球状机器人系列，其中20腿实体变体实现了近极限动态各向同性，展示了方向无关运动、复杂地形穿越、快速自稳定及部分执行器故障下的韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人对称性设计仅停留在几何形态层面，缺乏从动态驱动能力层面统一利用对称性的方法论。作者旨在探索动态层面的对称性是否能为机器人带来更根本的性能提升。

Method: 1. 提出"动态对称性"概念并形式化为"动态各向同性"指标；2. 在超1000种仿真形态中进行大规模分析；3. 设计Argus机器人系列，基于径向线性致动器直接调控质心动力学，逐步逼近动态各向同性的理论极限；4. 构建20腿实体原型并实验验证。

Result: 高动态对称性一致改善轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效；20腿Argus实现近极限动态各向同性，表现出方向无关运动、复杂地形适应性、快速自稳定和执行器故障容错能力，并具备全向感知与交互能力。

Conclusion: 不仅在形态层面，更在可达动力学层面追求对称性，是通往不确定性环境下机器人敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性的通用有效途径。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [5] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 是运动规划库的重大演进，通过硬件加速实现实时运动规划，并与现代AI研究工作流无缝集成。该库自2008年发布以来，持续扩展了新的规划器、状态空间和问题形式化，成为采样运动规划领域的基石。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近二十年的持续发展中，运动规划领域和OMPL库共同成长，需要将库升级以支持实时运动规划和AI研究工作流，同时反映领域与库的协同演化。

Method: OMPL 2.0 通过硬件加速实现实时运动规划，集成现代AI研究工作流，并在原有基础上新增了渐近最优规划器、惰性规划器、约束运动规划以及时序逻辑目标规划等功能。

Result: OMPL 2.0 实现了从离线规划到实时运动规划的跨越，提供了与AI研究生态兼容的接口，并保持了作为运动规划领域标准库的地位。

Conclusion: OMPL 与运动规划领域在过去近二十年间相互促进、共同发展。OMPL 2.0 的发布标志着运动规划库向实时化和AI融合方向的重要演进，对研究社区产生了广泛影响。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [6] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双无人机空中对接平台，两架四旋翼飞行器采用主从编队，通过被动磁吸式轻量化模块完成空中对接，并设计了进度感知的任务管理器来管理四个阶段（接近、对齐、捕获、稳定），在仿真和实物实验中验证了平台的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机间的可靠空中对接是实现模块化空中协作与操控的关键，但受限于推力、载荷约束以及对相对位姿控制的高精度要求，现有方案缺乏可重复且可基准评估的验证平台。

Method: 采用主从编队（leader-follower）的两架四旋翼飞行器，搭配被动磁吸式轻量化对接模块，设计进度感知任务管理器（approach, alignment, capture, settle四个阶段），集成ROS 2与Crazyflie/PX4接口的完整软硬件栈，并实现同步日志记录用于基准评估。

Result: 在仿真和实物实验中，通过编队误差、基线/偏航一致性、对接成功率、对接耗时、失败模式统计等定量指标对平台进行了评估，证明了平台的有效性。

Conclusion: 该平台为对接监控与同步策略提供了可统计比较的基准，为模块化空中协作和可重复的空中操控搭建了实用的测试平台。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [7] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DVLA是一种即插即用框架，通过多视角一致性约束、实例估计模块和遮挡处理机制，为预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型注入鲁棒的3D推理能力，显著提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解，表现为三个相互关联的挑战：弱3D空间位置提取（缺乏多视角一致性约束）、不足的3D实例理解、以及遮挡下的脆弱推理。直接将成熟3D感知方法集成到VLA管线中，面临架构不兼容和对昂贵实例级标注的严重依赖问题。

Method: 3DVLA包含三大模块：(1) 贯穿所有模态的显式多视角一致性约束的3D特征编码，以及空间条件几何聚合方法；(2) 带有高层级实例令牌的实例估计模块，实现3D实例感知；(3) 掩码自监督3D编码分支，保留预测器用于视觉令牌补全以处理遮挡。

Result: 将3DVLA集成到多个VLA基线中，在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0上评估，操作性能取得一致且显著的提升，验证了方法的有效性和即插即用兼容性。

Conclusion: 3DVLA是一种无需额外人工标注、不丢弃VLM先验知识的即插即用框架，有效解决了VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解的三个关键挑战，并在多个基线和数据集上验证了其通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [8] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA提出了面向VLA模型的阶段自适应推理框架，通过轻量调度器动态分配视觉编码器、大语言模型和动作头的计算资源，在不修改基础模型的情况下实现高达3.77倍加速，并将控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型计算成本高、控制频率受限，且现有加速方法通常对每个控制步骤采用固定计算策略，忽视了序列化具身控制中非均匀的推理需求，受人类运动中认知资源集中在目标敏感阶段的现象启发，作者提出VLA模型应学会何时投入完整计算、何时复用先前计算。

Method: 设计轻量调度器，联合观察时序表示相似性、机器人运动线索和任务进度，统一调度三个模块：(1) 视觉-语言推理部分，根据表示稳定性选择五级计算模式（从完全重计算到多步时序复用）；(2) 动作生成部分，根据运动稳定性选择三级去噪模式（稳定阶段复用中间去噪状态，目标敏感阶段保留完整优化）。作为即插即用框架，无需修改或重训练基础模型。

Result: 在GR00T和CogACT上分别实现2.55倍和3.77倍加速；在六项真实世界GR00T任务中，计算量减少2.18倍，控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA是一种通用的VLA加速框架，通过动态计算调度有效平衡了推理效率与控制性能，特别适用于具有显式动作生成模块的现代VLA流水线。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [9] [Human-in-the-Loop Swarms: A Bionic Swarm Approach to Real-World Soil Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29091)
*Petras Swissler,Mohammadali Rashidioun,Nicholas Sahu,Raaid Kabir,Ayodeji Aderibigbe,Oladoyin Kolawole*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了“Bionic Swarm”系统，通过将机器人难以实现但对算法评估无直接贡献的任务交给人类用户（即人在环中），降低了群体和现场机器人技术在实际验证中的门槛。该系统使用智能手机Web应用接收蓝牙传感器的测量数据，传输到中央服务器运行群体算法并指导人类行动。通过Score-Biased-Search算法的实验验证，展示了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体和现场机器人技术在现实世界验证中面临硬件成本高和开发时间长的重大障碍，需要一种更低门槛的验证方法。

Method: 提出Bionic Swarm系统，采用人在环中的方法：人类用户通过智能手机Web应用接收指令，利用蓝牙传感器采集数据并上传至中央服务器，服务器运行群体算法后反馈行动指令给人类用户。

Result: 通过Score-Biased-Search算法的仿真和户外实地实验验证，证明了该系统能够有效降低群体机器人研究的入门门槛。

Conclusion: 人在环中的方法可以显著降低现场和群体机器人研究的准入门槛，为难以在真实硬件上快速部署的算法提供了一种可行的验证途径。

Abstract: Swarm and field robotics face significant barriers to real-world validation due to the high cost and development time to deploy hardware. This paper introduces the ``Bionic Swarm,'' a novel system that lowers these barriers by abstracting away many of the tasks that are difficult to implement on robots but which do not contribute to the overall algorithm evaluation, giving these tasks to human users. These human users take directions from a smartphone web-app that takes measurements from Bluetooth-connected sensors and relays them to a centralized server. This server runs the swarm algorithm and directs actions to the human users. We evaluate this system through the experimental validation of a geotechnically-focused search algorithm named Score-Biased-Search, which functions by assigning a ``score'' to each location on a reconstructed map, then biases search patterns through areas of higher expected scores, and which exhibits superlinear map reconstruction relative to the number of search agents. After presenting simulation results for the algorithm, we then apply the algorithm on the Bionic Swarm platform to validate its function in a real-world, outdoor setting. This work demonstrates that this human-in-the-loop approach significantly lowers the barrier to entry for field and swarm robotics research.

</details>


### [10] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-Seg是一个针对越野环境语义分割的框架，通过风格扩展和纹理正则化来应对分布偏移，显著提升了模型在多种目标域上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境中的语义分割面临源-目标域差异和传感器因粗糙地形导致的损坏，造成分布偏移，使模型预测不准确，导致导航任务失败。现有方法隐式地在固定源分布内进行泛化，效果有限。

Method: 提出ST-Seg框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 风格扩展(SE)：通过生成多样化的逼真风格来扩宽域覆盖，增强源域的有限风格信息；2) 纹理正则化(TR)：通过深度纹理流形稳定受风格增强学习影响的局部纹理表示。

Result: 在多种存在分布偏移的目标域上进行实验，ST-Seg相比现有方法取得了显著的性能提升，证明了其在越野导航语义分割中的鲁棒性和实际应用价值。

Conclusion: ST-Seg通过直观的分布偏移处理方式（风格扩展+纹理正则化），有效提升了越野环境下语义分割的鲁棒性和实际可用性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [11] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLAConf框架，利用冻结的VLA内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步骤异常分数，实现高效且通用的置信度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型置信度估计方法计算效率低且难以泛化到不同架构，尤其是连续动作空间，需要更高效通用的解决方案。

Method: VLAConf采用单类判别置信度框架，利用冻结的预训练VLA内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步骤异常分数，并采用步骤条件建模编码操作轨迹中的 rollout 阶段信息。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，VLAConf显著提升了后验校准中置信度信号的质量，推理效率大幅优于现有基线，并在真实机器人实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: VLAConf是一种高效、通用且可泛化的VLA模型置信度估计方法，适用于连续动作空间，显著提高了推理效率和置信度信号质量。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [12] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MARS策略，自适应地在需要多模态的阶段注入噪声，在单模态阶段使用高效确定性学习，从而在保持多模态表达能力的同时大幅提升训练和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式策略通过随机噪声初始化和迭代去噪实现多模态，导致训练复杂度高、推理效率低；而机器人任务中并非所有阶段都需要行为多样性，因此希望在需要时激活多模态，不需要时退化为确定性学习。

Method: Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) 策略，自适应地在真正需要行为多样性的阶段注入适当噪声，在单模态阶段切换为高效确定性学习，桥接生成式策略的多模态能力与确定性模型的高效性。

Result: 在8个模拟任务和4个真实世界任务上验证：真实测试中成功率提升16.67%，推理延迟降低83.20%；在近确定性任务上训练效率甚至超过确定性策略。

Conclusion: MARS策略通过选择性激活多模态生成，有效兼顾了多模态表达能力和训练推理效率，为机器人模仿学习提供了更实用的范式。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a cornerstone for solving complex robotic manipulation tasks. In particular, multimodality, which enables robots to capture diverse yet valid behavioral patterns, has driven the rapid emergence of generative policies as a dominant paradigm in robot learning. However, achieving such multimodality typically relies on stochastic noise initialization and iterative denoising procedures, resulting in substantial training complexity and low inference efficiency. Meanwhile, not all phases of a robotic task inherently require behavioral diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) policy, which adaptively invokes tailored stochasticity only when it is truly beneficial, while reverting to an efficient deterministic learning during single-modal phases. In other words, the proper amount of noise is injected only at the proper time. By selectively activating multimodal generation, MARS policy bridges the gap between the multimodal capability of generative policies and the superior training and inference efficiency of deterministic models. Empirical studies across 8 simulated and 4 real-world tasks demonstrate that MARS exhibits robust multimodal expressivity and high efficiency, with a 16.67% success rate improvement and an 83.20% inference latency reduction in real-world tests. Counterintuitively, MARS also outpaces deterministic policies in training efficiency on near-deterministic tasks by more effectively modeling nuanced action diversity.

</details>


### [13] [Fisher-Preserving Guidance: Training-Free Manifold Constraints for Safe Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29937)
*Hao Ren,Zetong Bi,Yiming Zeng,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Zhaoliang Wan,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过低秩Jacobian分解计算Fisher保持更新，避免扩散模型在视觉导航路径预测中偏离训练流形，并在多个基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉导航路径预测中有效，但标准采样和测试时引导会产生不可靠或低效轨迹，因为更新会偏离训练流形。

Method: 提出Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection：通过低秩Jacobian分解计算Fisher保持更新（仅需单次反向传播），避免大Fisher漂移；引入Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity作为不确定性信号，用于鲁棒的多样本动作混合。

Result: 在Maze2D、PushT、仿真和真实机器人视觉导航等基准上，相比强扩散策略基线取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需额外训练即可在推理时提升扩散策略的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion models are effective for waypoint prediction in visual navigation, but standard sampling and test time guidance can produce unreliable or inefficient trajectories when updates drift off the training manifold. We propose Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection, a training-free inference method that avoids large Fisher drift associated with off-distribution actions while optimizing a task objective. Our method computes the Fisher-preserving update via a low-rank Jacobian factorization, requiring only a single backward pass per step and enabling real-time use. We further introduce Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity as an uncertainty signal and use it for robust multi-sample action blending. Experiments on toy and realistic navigation benchmarks, including Maze2D with TSDF-based guidance, PushT with official Diffusion Policy weights, and visual navigation in simulation and on real robots, demonstrate consistent improvements in performance over strong diffusion-policy baselines without additional training.

</details>


### [14] [Replicable Simulation-Based Robot Validation through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29973)
*Argentina Ortega,Samuel Wiest,Frederik Pasch,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将数据溯源与FAIR原则集成到机器人仿真测试流程中，通过追踪工件间链接和附加机器可读元数据来提升测试的可复现性，并在移动机器人导航数据集上进行了验证，最后讨论了集成过程中的障碍与建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真测试的可复现性依赖于测试配置、执行和后处理过程的透明文档化，而现有方法缺乏对数据来源和关键设计决策的显式追踪。

Method: 在现有仿真测试框架中增强溯源追踪和元数据收集机制，将溯源与FAIR元数据集成到测试流程中，而非事后附加到最终数据集。

Result: 成功扩展了移动机器人导航数据集，使其包含结构化溯源信息和符合FAIR原则的元数据，实现了端到端的证据重建。

Conclusion: 数据溯源与FAIR元数据必须嵌入测试流程本身；集成过程中面临词汇对齐、属性选择、领域标准采纳等挑战，并提出了相应的 actionable 建议。

Abstract: Robot behavior is often validated through simulation-based testing, yet the replicability of such campaigns depends critically on transparent documentation of how tests are configured, executed, and post-processed. We argue that data provenance, coupled with the FAIR principles (findability, accessibility, interoperability, and reusability), addresses this gap by explicitly tracking links between artifacts and by attaching machine-readable metadata about file origins and key design decisions. Moreover, provenance and metadata cannot be treated as an afterthought confined to final datasets; they must be integrated into the testing processes that generate those datasets so that evidence can be reconstructed end-to-end. We demonstrate this by augmenting an existing simulation-based testing framework with provenance tracking and metadata collection mechanisms, and by using these extensions to enrich a mobile robot navigation dataset with structured provenance and FAIR-aligned metadata. Finally, we discuss obstacles encountered in this integration -- such as vocabulary alignment, attribute selection, and adoption of domain standards -- and provide actionable recommendations for implementing provenance-centric, FAIR metadata in robotics validation workflows.

</details>


### [15] [BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yifan Han,Yanming Shao,Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BORA是一个离线到在线的强化学习后训练框架，通过冻结VLA基础模型并引入轻量级的人机协作分块残差适应机制，有效解决了高维灵巧手操作中的时序不一致、样本低效和硬件风险问题，在五个真实世界灵巧任务上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在灵巧操作中面临高维手部控制和执行误差累积的挑战，真实世界的RL后训练虽有必要，但高维探索会导致时序不一致、样本低效和硬件风险。

Method: 离线阶段：构建以VLM认知token和动作块为输入的Critic，实现动作条件化价值引导；在线阶段：冻结VLA基座，引入人机协作分块残差适应机制来修正执行误差和离线学到的意图，并利用干预驱动奖励进行校正。

Result: 在五个复杂真实世界灵巧任务上，BORA在标准设置下平均成功率提升33%，在未见物体泛化场景下提升43%。

Conclusion: BORA通过离线Critic继承和在线人机协作残差适应，有效弥合了视觉引导动作生成与物理可靠执行之间的差距，同时保持了预训练策略的稳定先验。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for grounding visual-language understanding into real-world robotic manipulation. However, dexterous manipulation remains challenging for VLA policies due to high-dimensional hand control and compounding execution errors, which makes real-world RL post-training essential for bridging the gap between visually grounded action generation and physically reliable dexterous execution. However, high-dimensional dexterous exploration often triggers temporal inconsistency, sample inefficiency and hardware risks in the real world. To address these challenges, we propose BORA, an offline-to-online RL post-training framework designed for real-world dexterous VLA models. In the offline phase, BORA constructs a critic that takes both the VLM's cognition tokens and action chunks as inputs. This design enables action-conditioned value guidance, allowing the critic to evaluate dexterous hand motions beyond visual context alone. During the subsequent online phase, BORA freezes the VLA base and introduces a lightweight, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) chunk-wise residual adaptation mechanism to mitigate real-world execution errors and further correct the offline-learned intents within the actual physical environment. By inheriting the offline critic and employing intervention-driven rewards, BORA effectively corrects execution discrepancies and adapts to real-world physical variances while preserving the pretrained policy as a stable prior. Extensive evaluations across five complex real-world dexterous tasks demonstrate that BORA significantly outperforms pure imitation learning and traditional decoupled RL baselines, achieving a 33% absolute increase in average success rate under standard settings and up to a 43% improvement in unseen object generalization.

</details>


### [16] [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280)
*Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Jian Guan,Jinhui Ye,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Jie Zhang,Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Pei Lin,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Shuai Bai,Jingren Zhou,Jiazhao Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Gengze Zhou,Hang Yin,Ye Wang,Yiyang Huang,Zixing Lei,Wujian Peng,Delin Chen,Yingming Zheng,Jingyang Fan,Xianwei Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Haoyang Li,Anzhe Chen,Tong Zhang,Xuejing Liu,Yuchong Sun,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Chenxu Lü,Zhibo Yang,Tao Yu,Xionghui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Qwen-VLA是一个统一的具身基础模型，将异构的视觉-语言-动作决策问题统一到一个框架中，通过DiT动作解码器实现从感知到连续动作生成的端到端建模，在操作、导航和轨迹预测等多个具身任务上展现了跨本体、跨场景的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能研究通常针对单个任务（如操作或导航）设计专门模型，导致能力碎片化，难以在不同任务、环境和机器人本体之间实现泛化。本文旨在探索是否可以将异构的具身决策问题统一到一个视觉-语言-动作模型中。

Method: Qwen-VLA基于Qwen的视觉-语言建模栈，扩展了DiT（扩散Transformer）动作解码器以支持连续动作和轨迹生成。采用大规模联合预训练策略，融合多种数据源。引入具身感知提示条件化，通过机器人特定文本描述指定当前本体和控制约定。将操作、导航和轨迹预测统一为动作-轨迹预测框架。

Result: LIBERO: 97.9%, Simpler-WidowX: 73.7%, RoboTwin-Easy/Hard: 86.1%/87.2%, R2R OSR: 69.0%, RxR SR: 59.6%, 真实ALOHA平均OOD成功率: 76.9%, DOMINO零样本成功率: 26.6%。

Conclusion: Qwen-VLA在多种操作、导航和轨迹中心基准上展现了持续的多任务性能和分布外泛化能力，能够适应场景布局、背景、光照、物体配置和机器人本体的变化，验证了将异构具身决策问题统一到单一框架的可行性。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.

</details>


### [17] [Gaze2Act: Gaze-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Interactive Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30282)
*Kuangji Zuo,Gen Li,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Boyu Ma,Shijia Han,Xinyu Zhou,Xichen Yuan,Chuhao Zhou,Jiaqi Bai,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaze2Act框架，利用人类凝视作为动态直观的意图信号，结合跨视角语义匹配和粗到细的目标指定方法，在人形机器人上实现了优于基线方法的意图准确率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠语言指令难以精确传达人类意图，尤其是在区分相似物体、指定交互位置以及应对动态变化场景时存在明显不足，需要更自然的意图表达方式。

Method: Gaze2Act首先通过跨视角语义匹配将第一人称凝视映射到机器人视角，生成物体掩码和凝视点以实现粗到细的目标指定；然后将这些线索通过感知层提示和动作层条件化融入策略中，使机器人能够关注相关区域并在动态意图下执行精确交互。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上跨越7个任务类别和16个真实机器人任务进行系统评估，在意图准确率和任务成功率上均达到最优性能，在物体区分、细粒度交互和动态意图引导方面显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 人类凝视是一种自然、低负担且表达能力强的模态，可用于人在回路中的VLA控制，有效弥补了纯语言指令在意图传达上的不足。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown strong potential for robot learning by following language instructions. However, in practice, language alone is often insufficient to precisely convey human intent. It is difficult to describe which exact object to interact with among similar candidates, where to act on the object, or how the target may change during execution. To address this limitation, we propose Gaze2Act, a novel VLA framework that leverages human gaze as a dynamic and intuitive intent signal for complex interactive manipulation. Gaze2Act first bridges the ego-exo view gap by mapping first-person gaze into the robot's perspective through cross-view semantic matching, producing both an object mask and a gaze point for coarse-to-fine target specification. These cues are then integrated into the policy through perception-level prompting and action-level conditioning, allowing the robot to attend to relevant regions and execute precise interactions under dynamic intent. In a systematic evaluation across seven task categories and 16 real-robot tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, Gaze2Act achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intent accuracy and task success rate. It notably outperforms baselines in object disambiguation, fine-grained interaction, and dynamic intent steering. These results demonstrate that human gaze provides a natural, low-burden, and highly expressive modality for human-in-the-loop VLA control.

</details>


### [18] [A Heterogeneous Architecture for Robot RL Beyond GPU-Dominant Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30313)
*Yufei Jia,Zhanxiang Cao,Mingrui Yu,Heng Zhang,Shenyu Chen,Dixuan Jiang,Meng Li,Xiaofan Li,Yiyang Liu,Junzhe Wu,Zheng Li,XiLin Fang,Tingyu Cui,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Anqi Wang,Zifan Wang,Dongjie Zhu,Chenyu Cao,Zhenbiao Huang,Ziang Zheng,Jie Lu,Xin Ma,Zhengyang Wei,Xiang Zhao,Tianyue Zhan,Ye He,Yuxiang Chen,Yizhou Jiang,Yue Li,Haizhou Ge,Yuhang Dong,Fan Jia,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Xiwa Deng,Zhixing Chen,Hanyang Shao,Chenxin Dong,Yixuan Li,Yizhi Chen,Bokui Chen,Kaifeng Zhang,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Xiang Li,Yue Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLab是一个异构CPU仿真/GPU学习的机器人强化学习训练系统，通过解耦CPU并行仿真与GPU策略更新，在相同硬件配置下实现3-10倍端到端训练效率提升，并支持跨平台执行（macOS、AMD ROCm、Intel XPU），证明GPU仿真并非高效训练的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于仿真的机器人控制训练普遍默认GPU物理仿真为高效训练的前提条件，本文旨在挑战这一假设，验证CPU仿真与GPU学习结合的异构架构能否达到同等或更优的训练效率。

Method: 提出UniLab异构架构，将CPU并行仿真与GPU策略更新解耦，通过统一运行时管理数据移动、缓冲和同步；使用MuJoCoUni和MotrixSim CPU批处理物理后端；支持PPO、SAC、FlashSAC、TD3、APPO等多种算法。

Result: 在代表性仿真机器人控制任务上，UniLab在相同硬件配置下实现3-10倍端到端训练效率提升；减少对NVIDIA CUDA软件栈依赖；支持macOS、AMD ROCm、Intel XPU等跨平台执行。

Conclusion: GPU仿真是高效训练的有效路径，但并非必要路径，UniLab拓宽了机器人强化学习训练的实际系统选择范围。

Abstract: Simulation-based RL for contemporary robot control is increasingly organized around GPU-resident simulation: physics, rollout collection, and learning are placed on a single GPU-centric execution path. This paradigm has greatly improved training speed, but it has also encouraged a default assumption that efficient training requires physics to reside on the GPU. We revisit this assumption. Our view is that, in simulation-dominated robot control, the essential question is not which processor runs physics, but whether simulation throughput, policy learning, and runtime synchronization form an efficient end-to-end loop. We present UniLab, a heterogeneous CPU-simulation / GPU-learning architecture that decouples CPU-parallel simulation from GPU policy updates through a unified runtime for data movement, buffering, and synchronization. UniLab is implemented as a complete and extensible training system using MuJoCoUni and MotrixSim CPU-batched physics backends, supporting PPO, SAC, FlashSAC, TD3, and APPO. On representative simulation-based robot control tasks, UniLab improves end-to-end training efficiency by 3--10$\times$ under the same hardware configuration, while reducing dependence on the NVIDIA CUDA-based software stack and supporting cross-platform execution on the Apple macOS platform and the AMD ROCm and Intel XPU accelerator backends. These results show that GPU simulation is an effective path to efficient training, but not a necessary one, broadening the practical system choices available for robot RL training. Project page: https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab.

</details>


### [19] [RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326)
*Chunru Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Fenghao Yu,Zhehuan Chen,Thomas L. Griffiths,Yejin Choi,David Held,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWits是一个双臂机器人基准测试，通过自动化任务生成管道创建包含推理、创意工具使用和应对意外情况的评估场景，揭示了预训练VLA在需要推理和策略适应的操作任务中的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试主要关注技能级执行，缺乏对机器人在真实环境中推理、适应和创造性解决问题等认知能力的系统性评估。

Method: 提出RoboWits双臂机器人基准测试，采用多智能体协作框架的自动化任务生成管道，包含种子任务生成与验证、度量生成、场景生成和任务变异等智能体，构建了30个种子任务和208个变异任务，涵盖几何、材料和装配推理难度分级。

Result: 预训练VLA在单任务微调后能在种子任务上取得初步成功，但在变异任务上表现挣扎，揭示了其在需要推理、策略适应以及对欺骗性或受限环境鲁棒性的操作任务中的脆弱性。

Conclusion: RoboWits基准测试揭示了当前VLA在认知推理和策略适应方面的显著性能差距，为评估机器人认知推理能力提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits.

</details>

### [20] [Runtime Analysis of a Compact Genetic Algorithm on a Truly Multi-valued OneMax Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29477)
*Martin S. Krejca,Carsten Witt*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文将多值紧凑遗传算法(cGA)在G-OneMax函数上的运行时间复杂度从O(n r³ log²(n) log(r))改进到O(n r log³(n) log³(r))，通过改进漂移定理和浓度不等式分析概率质量在频率矩阵中的移动过程，使得结果匹配更简单双值情形下的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多值分布估计算法运行时间分析几乎都局限于每个维度只区分两种类别的多值目标函数，而Adak和Witt (GECCO 2025)首次对真正依赖全部r类别的G-OneMax函数进行了分析，但其结果仍有待改进。

Method: 采用改进的漂移定理处理高自环概率过程，并推导专门的浓度不等式，分析多值cGA中概率质量如何逐次收缩到r值频率矩阵的越来越小的区间内。

Result: 将运行时间上界从O(n r³ log²(n) log(r))改进到O(n r log³(n) log³(r))，在多项式对数因子内匹配了更简单的双值OneMax函数的已知界。

Conclusion: 通过优化更新强度K的选择和运用新的分析技术，显著提升了多值cGA在G-OneMax上的运行时间界，缩小了与双值情形之间的理论差距。

Abstract: Recently, the runtime analysis of multi-valued estimation-of-distribution algorithms in the framework of Ben Jedidia et al. (TCS 2024) has made significant advancements. However, almost all existing analyses are limited to multi-valued objective functions that in each dimension only distinguish between two types, also called categories, of values and hence can be treated with similar methods as pseudo-Boolean problems. Only recently, Adak and Witt (GECCO 2025) have presented a first runtime analysis of a multi-valued compact genetic algorithm (cGA) on the multi-valued OneMax function G-OneMax$\colon \{0,\dots,r-1\}^n \to \mathbf{N}$ defined by G-OneMax$(x_1,\dots,x_n)=\sum_{i=1}^n {x}_i$ and truly depending on all $r$ categories. We improve their runtime result from $\textrm{O}\bigl(n r^3 \log^2( n)\log (r)\bigr)$ to $\textrm{O}\bigl(n r \log^3(n)\log^3(r)\bigr)$, both for an optimal choice of the update strength $K$. Our result matches, up to polylogarithmic factors, the existing bound for the simpler $r$-valued OneMax function depending essentially only on two values and analyzed in several previous works. To show the new bound, we use improved drift theorems for processes with high self-loop probabilities and specifically derived concentration inequalities to analyze how probability mass in the multi-valued cGA moves into successively smaller and smaller intervals of the $r$-valued frequency matrix.

</details>


### [21] [WASHH: An Anchor-Aware Whale-Guided Selection Hyper-Heuristic for Continuous Optimization and SVC Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28844)
*Yifu Zhao,Xiaofan Zou,Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Baili Lu,Zhenhong Peng,Dexing Yao,Haochen Li,Qinbin He,Sio-Kei Im,Xu Yang,Yapeng Wang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出WASHH，一种鲸鱼引导的自适应选择超启发式算法，用于连续黑箱优化。它集成多种搜索行为（PSO、GWO、DE、局部坐标搜索、锚点引导精化）并在线分配评估预算。在10个30维基准函数上取得平均排名1.10，并在乳腺癌诊断SVC超参数配置任务中以300次评估取得最低验证对数损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习辅助的算法设计通常需要在较小的评估预算下做出可靠的搜索决策，而单一元启发式算法的可靠性不足。

Method: WASHH以WOA为主要开发骨架，将PSO式记忆、GWO式领导者平均、DE式变异、局部坐标搜索和锚点引导精化作为可选搜索行为，通过在线奖励控制器根据观测到的改进分配评估资源。锚点精化利用廉价参考配置（如盒中心或默认模型设置）而不绕过黑箱评估。

Result: 在10个30维基准函数上，10次独立运行、12,000次评估下，WASHH取得最佳平均排名1.10，在所有10个函数上均为最佳或并列最佳。在Rastrigin和Griewank上达到数值最优并持平WOA，在其他8个函数上严格优于WOA。在300次评估预算的乳腺癌SVC超参数配置中取得最低平均验证对数损失。

Conclusion: 锚点感知的选择超启发式是LEAD系统的一个实用的轻量级方向。

Abstract: Learning-assisted algorithm design often has to make reliable search decisions under small evaluation budgets, where committing to a single metaheuristic can be unreliable. We propose WASHH, a Whale-guided Adaptive Selection Hyper-Heuristic for continuous black-box optimization. WASHH uses WOA as the main exploitation backbone, but treats PSO-style memory, GWO-style leader averaging, DE-style variation, local coordinate search, and anchor-guided refinement as selectable search behaviors. An online reward controller allocates evaluations according to observed improvements, while anchor refinement exploits inexpensive reference configurations such as box centers or default model settings without bypassing black-box evaluation. On ten 30-dimensional benchmark functions with 10 independent runs and 12,000 evaluations, WASHH achieves the best average rank, 1.10, and is best or tied best on all ten functions. It strictly improves over WOA on eight functions and ties WOA at the numerical optimum on Rastrigin and Griewank. We further study SVC hyperparameter configuration for breast cancer diagnosis under a 300-evaluation budget. WASHH obtains the lowest mean validation log loss among the compared optimizers, suggesting that anchor-aware selection hyper-heuristics are a practical lightweight direction for LEAD systems.

</details>

### [22] [Inferring Code Correctness from Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29822)
*Tambon Florian,Papadakis Mike*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TRAILS方法，通过基于规约生成测试输入、执行代码并让LLM评估输入-输出对是否符合规约（不推理代码本身）来验证LLM生成代码的正确性，在多个数据集和模型上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码缺乏有效的正确性验证方法，现有方法要么依赖多候选动态共识（成本高、难扩展），要么依赖静态推理（易受动态错误和顺序偏差影响）。

Method: TRAILS首先基于规约通过类别划分生成多样化的测试输入，然后在候选代码上执行这些输入，最后让LLM评估生成的输入-输出对是否符合规约（不推理代码本身），跨输入聚合评分以判断程序正确性。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CoCoClaNeL两个数据集、三个LLM上评估，TRAILS相比Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought将Matthews相关系数提升高达39%，一致优于HoarePrompt；同时展现出更高的稳定性，减少对LLM非确定性的敏感度。

Conclusion: TRAILS通过将LLM推理锚定在具体的(输入, 输出)对上，有效提升了代码正确性验证的准确性和稳定性，并且能够正确标记更多独特的代码样本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become integral to modern software development, enabling automated code generation at scale. However, validating the correctness of LLM-generated code remains a critical and largely unsolved challenge. Existing approaches either rely on dynamic consensus across multiple code candidates - making them costly and difficult to scale - or on static reasoning that is susceptible to dynamic bugs and order bias. In this paper, we propose TRAILS~ (Targeted Reasoning Agreement via Inputs and Specifications), an approach that grounds LLM reasoning with concrete (input, output) pairs. TRAILS~ first generates diverse test inputs via category partitioning based on the specification, then executes them against the candidate code and prompts LLMs to assess whether the resulting input-output pairs conform to the specification - without ever reasoning over the code itself. Scores are aggregated across inputs, to determines whether the program is likely correct. We evaluate TRAILS~ on two datasets, LiveCodeBench and CoCoClaNeL, across three LLMs (Qwen3Coder-30B, Devstral-Small-24B, and Olmo3.1-Instruct), comparing against HoarePrompt and a Zero-Shot Chain-of-Thought baseline. TRAILS~ improves Matthew Correlation Coefficient by up to 39\% relative to Zero-Shot COT and consistently outperforms HoarePrompt. Beyond accuracy, TRAILS~ demonstrates greater stability across seeded runs, reducing sensitivity to LLM non-determinism, and assigns correct labels to a larger set of unique code samples than competing approaches.

</details>


### [23] [How Coding Agents Fail Their Users: A Large-Scale Analysis of Developer-Agent Misalignment in 20,574 Real-World Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29442)
*Ningzhi Tang,Chaoran Chen,Gelei Xu,Yiyu Shi,Yu Huang,Collin McMillan,Tao Dong,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对20,574个编码智能体会话进行观察研究，从IDE和CLI工作流中识别出七种反复出现的失调模式，发现90.50%的失调造成信任和精力成本而非不可逆损害，但91.49%仍需用户显式纠正；失调模式在不同环境下存在差异且随时间演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖基准轨迹分析智能体故障，但忽略了开发者在实际工作流中体验失调的方式，缺乏对编码智能体真实失败模式的系统性理解。

Method: 对IDE和CLI工作流中来自1,639个代码仓库的20,574个编码智能体会话进行观察研究，将失调操作为通过开发者推回（pushback）可见的断裂，并沿形式、原因、成本、解决四个维度进行标注。

Result: 识别出七种失调形式；90.50%的失调造成信任和精力成本而非系统不可逆损害；91.49%的可见解决仍需用户显式纠正；失调模式在IDE和CLI设置间存在差异，跨相邻会话持续存在，且随时间演变——总体率下降但约束违规和不准确自我报告占比上升。

Conclusion: 研究结果为训练、评估和接口设计提供指导，以保持编码智能体与真实开发者工作流对齐。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly act directly within software environments, yet existing analyses of their failures rely on benchmark trajectories that miss how developers actually experience misalignment. We present an observational study of 20,574 coding-agent sessions from 1,639 repositories across IDE and CLI workflows. We operationalize misalignment as a breakdown made visible through developer pushback, and annotate each episode along four axes: form, cause, cost, and resolution. We identify seven recurring forms, spanning how agents read projects, interpret developer intent, follow rules, bound their actions, implement and execute code, and report progress. 90.50\% of episodes impose effort and trust costs rather than irreversible system damage, yet 91.49\% of visible resolutions still require explicit user correction. Misalignment patterns also differ across IDE and CLI settings, persist across adjacent sessions, and shift over time: while overall rates decline, constraint violations and inaccurate self-reporting grow in share. Our findings inform the design of training, evaluation, and interfaces for keeping coding agents aligned with real developer workflows.

</details>


### [24] [Usability Analysis of Configurator User Interfaces with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29456)
*Sebastian Lubos,Alexander Felfernig,Damian Garber,Adnan Kraljić,Tarik Kraljić,Viet-Man Le,Thi Ngoc Trang Tran,Gerhard Leitner,Julian Schwazer,Doris Suppan,Reinhard Willfort,Ivan Dukic,Jeremias Fuchs,Manuel Henrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对配置器用户界面进行可扩展的半自动化可用性分析，综合18项配置器特定可用性标准，对16个真实配置器进行评估，发现MLLMs能可靠识别可用性问题并提供领域感知的改进建议，可显著降低人工分析成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置器应用的可用性分析缺乏领域特定标准和自动化工具支持，现有通用可用性启发式方法不足以应对配置器特有的交互复杂性，亟需可扩展的系统化分析方法。

Method: 从文献中综合提炼出18项配置器特定可用性标准，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对16个真实世界配置器进行逐项可用性评估，生成严重性评级和可操作的改进建议。

Result: MLLMs能够可靠地识别配置器特有的可用性问题，并提供具有领域意识的改进建议，且该方法能显著降低可用性分析所需的人力投入。

Conclusion: 基于MLLM的半自动化方法在配置器可用性分析中具有实际应用价值，尽管人工验证仍然必要，但该方法有望大幅减少分析所需的努力。

Abstract: Configuration is a key technology for tailoring complex software systems, services, and products. A successful application of configurators not only depends on technical correctness, performance, and domain modeling but also on their usability. While general usability heuristics are widely used, configurator-specific criteria and tool support for systematic user interface (UI) analysis are limited. This paper explores the use of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for scalable and semi-automated usability analysis of configurator UIs. We synthesize 18 configurator-specific usability criteria from the literature and apply these criteria in an MLLM-based analysis of 16 real-world configurators. Each criterion is assessed individually to generate severity ratings for usability issues and actionable improvement suggestions. A review of the results confirms that MLLMs can reliably identify configurator-specific usability issues and provide domain-aware improvement recommendations. Although human validation remains necessary, this approach has the potential to significantly reduce the required effort to analyze configurator usability.

</details>


### [25] [Projectional Decoding: Towards Semantic-Aware LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30054)
*Boqi Chen,José Antonio Hernández López,Aren A. Babikian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"投影解码"（projectional decoding）框架，通过在文本生成过程中同步维护部分图模型作为工件表征，将领域语义直接集成到LLM生成过程中，实现增量式语义验证，支持不确定性捕获和错误检测，从而保证生成输出的语义有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成软件工件的约束解码技术仅能保证句法正确性或特定语义规则，缺乏将生成文本与软件工程中所需的语义验证推理相连接的通用表示方法。

Method: 提出投影解码框架：在文本生成过程中，同时维护一个部分图模型作为主要的工件表征；该抽象表征能够显式捕获不确定性、支持增量式语义验证和错误检测，并引导生成过程朝向具有可证明保证的语义有效输出。

Result: 在程序生成任务上的初步实验结果表明，该方法有潜力提高LLM生成工件的语义有效性。

Conclusion: 投影解码能够在多种软件工程活动中实现基于LLM的可验证自动化，为连接文本生成与领域语义验证提供了一种通用的概念框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate software artifacts across many software engineering (SE) tasks, yet ensuring the semantic validity of these artifacts remains a fundamental challenge. Existing constrained decoding techniques can enforce syntactic correctness and, in some cases, specific semantic rules, but lack a general representation that bridges LLM-generated text with the reasoning required for semantic validation in SE. In this paper, we propose projectional decoding, a novel conceptual framework that integrates domain semantics directly into the generation process by maintaining, alongside text, a partial graph model as the primary artifact representation throughout generation. This abstract representation enables incremental semantic validation by explicitly capturing uncertainty and natively supporting error detection, while guiding generation toward semantically valid outputs with provable guarantees. We present preliminary results on a program generation task which demonstrate the potential of this approach to improve the semantic validity of LLM-generated artifacts. We also discuss how projectional decoding can enable verifiable automation with LLMs across various SE activities.

</details>

*Yujie Ma,Jialin Rong,Chenxi Yang,Lili Quan,Xiaofei Xie,Yongqiang Lyu,Qiang Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个LLM-in-the-loop漏洞数据集LLMCVE，包含从230个流行LLM组件中收集的2,888个漏洞源，经人工筛选得到205个严格符合定义的漏洞。分析发现LLM在漏洞中更多扮演目标或传播载体而非根因。基于该数据集评估现有智能体漏洞修复方法，发现LLM-in-the-loop漏洞比传统软件漏洞更难精准修复，尤其是提示注入漏洞的Pass@1率仅为28.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究多停留在传统软件层面，忽略了LLM实际带来的危害，缺乏对真实世界中LLM-in-the-loop漏洞的系统性理解与数据集支持。

Method: 从230个流行LLM组件收集2,888个多源漏洞，通过人工分析筛选出205个严格符合LLM-in-the-loop漏洞定义的样本，构建LLMCVE数据集；利用该数据集评估SWE-Agent等智能体漏洞修复方法的效果。

Result: LLM在漏洞中更多作为攻击目标或传播向量而非根因；现有智能体修复方法对LLM-in-the-loop漏洞的修复效果显著低于传统漏洞，提示注入漏洞Pass@1仅28.57%。

Conclusion: LLM-in-the-loop漏洞具有与传统漏洞不同的特征，现有自动化修复方法对其修复效果不足，需要针对这类漏洞设计专门的修复策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models(LLMs) have been actively integrated into modern software systems as critical components. LLM-in-the-loop vulnerabilities, where vulnerabilities are introduced by LLMs and their dependent downstream components, such as frameworks, introduce new risks. Although some benchmark datasets have been constructed to study the impact of such vulnerabilities, most works still remain at the analysis from the conventional software level, ignoring the harm actually caused by LLMs. Understanding real-world LLM-in-the-loop vulnerabilities is still an open problem. To address this gap, we build the first LLM-in-the-loop vulnerability dataset, LLMCVE, to facilitate the risk analysis of LLM-integrated software. To do so, we first collect 2,888 multi-source vulnerabilities across 230 popular LLM components. Then, through manual analysis, we identify 205 vulnerabilities that strictly fall under the concept of LLM-in-the-loop vulnerability. Through analysis, we found that LLMs more often play as targets or propagation vectors rather than the root cause of these vulnerabilities. Furthermore, based on LLMCVE, we evaluate the repairing capabilities of existing agent-based vulnerability repair methods, such as SWE-Agent. Experimental results demonstrate that compared to conventional software vulnerabilities, LLM-in-the-Loop vulnerabilities are more challenging to precisely fix, especially for those involving prompt injections where the Pass@1 rate is only 28.57%.

</details>

*Kaihua Qin,Dawn Song,Arthur Gervais*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SCDBench是一个面向LLM智能合约反编译的标准化基准，包含600个真实合约，通过四阶段评估（格式完整性、可编译性、ABI恢复、语义一致性）对前沿模型进行评测，发现最佳模型仅完美反编译42/600合约，语义一致性问题远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约反编译评估存在数据集狭窄、指标不一致、语义一致性检查有限的问题。随着LLM开始生成看似合理的源级Solidity代码，评估反编译器的质量愈发重要。

Method: 构建包含600个真实Solidity合约的数据集，配对的字节码输入、真实源码和可复现的语义检查点；设计四阶段累积评估流程（格式完整性→可编译性→ABI恢复→语义一致性差分回放）；在零样本反编译和零样本编译修复两种设置下评估Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5.3-Codex、GLM-5等前沿模型。

Result: 前沿LLM通常能生成结构化且可编译的Solidity代码，但语义一致性远未解决：最佳模型仅完美反编译42/600合约（7%）；引入同模型编译修复可显著提升性能且额外成本适中。

Conclusion: SCDBench为智能合约反编译提供了标准化、可复现的评估基础，有助于加速开发可靠的智能合约反编译器，以增强区块链安全性和透明度。

Abstract: Smart contract decompilation aims to recover high-level source code from bytecode, but evaluating decompilers remains difficult because existing studies use narrow datasets, inconsistent metrics, and limited semantic consistency checks. This gap is increasingly important as large language models (LLMs) begin to generate source-like Solidity that may compile and appear plausible, even when its semantics diverge from the original contract. We introduce SCDBench, a dataset and benchmark methodology for LLM-based smart contract decompilation. The dataset contains 600 real-world Solidity contracts with paired bytecode inputs, ground-truth source code, and replayable semantic checkpoints. SCDBench evaluates decompiler outputs through four cumulative stages: format completeness, compilability, Application Binary Interface (ABI) recovery, and semantic consistency via differential replay. We evaluate Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5.3-Codex, and GLM-5 in a zero-shot decompilation setting, including GLM-5 variants with and without extended reasoning and a zero-shot compilation-repair setting. The results show that frontier LLMs can often produce structured and compilable Solidity, but achieving semantic consistency remains far from solved: the best-performing frontier model perfectly decompiles only 42/600 contracts. We further show that introducing same-model compilation repair substantially improves performance at modest additional cost. SCDBench establishes a common ground for rigorous, reproducible evaluation and aims to accelerate the development of reliable smart contract decompilers for blockchain security and transparency.

</details>


### [28] [CODEFUSE-DEBENCH: An Empirical Study on Readability, Recompilability, and Functionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29490)
*Puzhuo Liu,Yuhan Huang,Jianlei Chi,Peng Di,Yu Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以可复用性为导向的二进制反编译评估范式DEBENCH，从可读性、可重编译性和功能性三个正交维度衡量反编译器质量。在240个原子测试函数、640个二进制文件上的实验表明：最佳反编译器-LLM组合的程序级行为重合度仅22.3%，精确stdout匹配仅1.2%；可读性与功能性存在冲突；反编译器引擎间的功能差异（20倍）远大于修复LLM间的差异（1.6倍）；失败模式分为语法噪声、类型系统崩溃（约占19%修复错误）和不可逆的上游信息丢失三类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反编译评估主要依赖语法相似性或单一维度的可读性指标，无法捕捉实际场景中反编译结果的可复用性，亟需一个能全面反映代码实际可用性的评估体系。

Method: 构建DEBENCH框架：包含240个原子测试函数（8个源文件，编译为640个二进制文件）；采用LLM-as-judge进行可读性评分（含18个子维度的URAF）、50次迭代预算下的编译-修复循环、基于Frida的差分动态追踪（程序级/函数级/指令级）；评估5个主流反编译器和3个修复LLM。

Result: 1）可复用性悬崖显著：程序级行为重合度22.3%，stdout精确匹配仅1.2%；2）可读性与功能性最佳设置冲突：-O3可读性最低但功能性最高，Clang可读性低于GCC但功能性高2.6倍；3）反编译器间功能差异（20倍）远大于LLM间差异（1.6倍）；4）失败类型包括语法噪声、类型系统崩溃（占修复错误约19%）和不可逆上游信息丢失。

Conclusion: 反编译评估需多维度衡量可复用性，反编译器引擎的改进比增大修复模型更为关键；当前反编译技术在保留程序语义方面仍有根本性局限，尤其在ARM64重定位惯用法和C++ ABI特征等场景下存在不可逆的信息损失。

Abstract: Binary decompilation aims to recover binaries into high-level source code, but existing evaluations mainly rely on syntactic similarity or single-axis readability metrics, which fail to capture practical reusability. We propose a reusability-driven evaluation paradigm that measures decompiler quality along three orthogonal dimensions: readability, recompilability, and functionality. We present DEBENCH, the first automated framework for multidimensional decompilation evaluation. DEBENCH contains 240 atomic test functions, organized into 8 source files and compiled into 640 binaries. It combines LLM-as-judge readability scoring with URAF (18 sub-dimensions), iterative compile-and-repair under a fixed 50-iteration budget, and Frida-based differential dynamic tracing at the program, function, and instruction levels. We evaluate five mainstream decompilers and three repair LLMs. Our study reveals four findings. First, the reusability cliff is steep: the best decompiler-LLM pair reaches 22.3% Exact+Partial program-level behavioral overlap but only 1.2% exact stdout match, nearly 50 points below recompilability. Second, settings that maximize readability do not maximize functionality: -O3 yields the lowest readability but the highest functionality, and Clang gives lower readability than GCC but 2.6x higher functionality. Third, cross-decompiler variation at the functional level is 20x, far larger than the 1.6x cross-LLM variation, showing that progress depends more on decompiler engines than larger repair models. Fourth, failures fall into three categories: syntactic noise, type-system collapse (about 19% of repair errors), and irreversible upstream losses such as ARM64 relocation idioms and C++ ABI features.

</details>

### [29] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现知识编辑方法（ROME和MEMIT）虽然改变不同事实，但都作用于相同的关键权重子集；通过训练二进制掩码可逆转80%以上编辑效果，并揭示编辑机制是通过抑制而非覆盖知识（通过消除后续层的过度注意力实现），这解释了编辑无法传播到相关事实的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME、MEMIT）主要通过修改MLP权重来更新事实关联，但评估仅关注输出行为，其内部工作机制尚不明确。本文旨在探究不同事实编辑是否共享共同机制，以及这种机制的具体运作方式。

Method: 训练一个紧凑的二进制掩码作用于被编辑的权重上，通过逆转编辑效果来识别关键的共享权重子集；分析掩码如何逆转编辑（发现其通过消除后续层的过度注意力）；通过注入掩码观察编辑成功率变化来验证机制必要性。

Result: 二进制掩码能逆转训练集80%和测试集70%以上的编辑效果；注入掩码使编辑成功率从98%降至38%；编辑机制是通过抑制知识而非覆盖知识，具体表现为消除后续层的过度注意力。

Conclusion: 编辑是通过抑制而非覆盖知识的方式运作，这解释了为何ROME和MEMIT无法将修改传播到相关事实；识别出的共享功能子空间可用于检测和防御不当编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [30] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出差异电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability）指标，通过分析Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct在科学问答任务上的微调，发现SFT虽然任务适应更快但导致更强的电路破坏和灾难性遗忘，而RL能保留更多基础电路但任务适应更慢，从 mechanistic 层面解释了RL在抗遗忘上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究从行为层面发现RL比SFT更不易引发灾难性遗忘，但缺乏机制层面的理解。本文旨在探究这一行为优势是否在内部计算电路层面有所体现，即RL是否能够更好地保留预训练模型的内部计算回路。

Method: 提出差异电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability）指标，在head级别衡量微调过程中电路退化的程度。使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型在科学问答任务上对比RL和SFT两种微调方法，分析电路保留与任务适应能力之间的权衡关系。

Result: 存在明确的机制权衡：SFT在目标任务上适应更快，但导致更严重的电路破坏和先前能力遗忘；RL保留了更大比例的基础电路，但任务适应速度较慢。

Conclusion: 电路保留可能是RL相比SFT更能抵抗灾难性遗忘的重要机制原因，为理解和改进LLM微调策略提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [31] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四人非完美信息卡牌游戏Big 2中构建了自我对弈强化学习框架，系统比较了多种算法，发现PPO在随机、贪婪和启发式对手下均优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q学习；适度的熵正则化和当前策略自我对弈能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完美信息多智能体游戏（隐藏信息、稀疏奖励、非平稳对手）是强化学习的重要测试场景，但缺乏系统性的受控对比研究。本文以Big 2为平台，旨在建立可控的实验框架来比较不同RL算法在复杂多智能体博弈中的表现。

Method: 构建Big 2的自我对弈RL框架，在统一的环境、输入表示、训练预算和评估协议下，对比PPO、蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning四种算法；同时研究熵正则化程度和三种自我对弈策略（当前策略、检查点策略、固定对手）的影响。

Result: PPO在各类对手下均优于其他算法；适度熵正则化可防止策略过度确定化而提升性能；当前策略自我对弈在有限预算下比检查点自我对弈和固定对手训练提供更强的课程学习效果。

Conclusion: Big 2是研究深度强化学习在非完美信息、多智能体交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集下的有效受控实验平台，当前策略自我对弈与PPO的组合在上述挑战中表现最佳。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [32] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow提出了一种基于Koopman动力学专家的流匹配方法，通过置信度感知的胜者全得机制实现模式专用化，有效缓解了标准流匹配中因单一全局向量场估计器导致的多模态时间序列频谱收缩问题，在多个基准上实现了SOTA性能，Context-FID提升15.6%，判别分数提升38.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列通常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学（如振荡和高频变化），而标准流匹配方法依赖单一有限容量的全局向量场估计器。在异构时间分布中，不同模态可能经过相近的流状态但需要不兼容的条件速度，导致单一估计器学习到过度平滑的局部输运场近似，造成分支特定动力学衰减、频谱失真和模式覆盖不足。

Method: 提出了PrismFlow方法：1）引入Koopman启发的动力学专家，在潜空间中学习局部非线性时间演化的线性近似残差修正；2）提出置信度感知的胜者全得（WTA）目标，仅更新与每个样本最对齐的专家梯度，鼓励模式专用化；3）采样时，选中的专家向全局输运场添加残差动力学修正，在保持流匹配稳定性的同时恢复细粒度高频时域结构。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准流匹配中的频谱收缩问题，Context-FID提升15.6%，判别分数提升38.6%，在低数据场景下保持鲁棒性，并在预测和插补任务中表现有效。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过基于Koopman动力学专家和置信度感知WTA机制的流匹配方法，成功解决了多模态时间序列生成中因单一全局向量场估计器导致的分支动力学衰减和频谱失真问题，为处理复杂时间序列数据提供了有效的生成框架。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>

*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏差和噪声三部分统计框架研究柏拉图表征假说（PRH），提出柏拉图对齐源于对象与属性的普遍关系（由线性表征假说LRH编码），发现稀疏自编码器提取的线性特征在跨模态对齐上优于密集表征，中心化和归一化可缓解模型间的偏差差异，而表征噪声与数据稀缺性相关（词频与对齐度正相关），最终提出了一个精炼LRH的统计模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同AI模型（不同架构、训练方式）的表征为何会趋向于对齐（柏拉图表征假说PRH），并试图从统计角度解析其背后的机制。

Method: 采用三部分统计框架：（1）信号：利用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征，验证LRH与PRH的关系；（2）偏差：通过中心化和归一化操作缓解架构差异带来的表征偏差；（3）噪声：分析LLM和文本嵌入模型中词频与对齐度的相关性，揭示数据稀缺性导致的噪声。

Result: 稀疏自编码器提取的线性表征在跨模态对齐上优于密集表征；中心化和归一化能一致提升跨模型对齐效果；词频与表征对齐度之间存在强且一致的正相关关系。

Conclusion: 提出了一个统计模型精炼了线性表征假说，能够解释来自不同现代AI架构的表征对齐现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>
